<!-- --><!-- -->

<!-- --><img _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" alt="SuperMemo Method" class="ng-star-inserted" src="../../../assets/images/frontpage/graph/9-3-1-en.webp"/>

<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-text">
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-title">
<h2 _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="color-orange">Learn fast and forget about forgetting</h2>
</div>
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-desc">
<p _ngcontent-my-app-c7="">Back in the late 1980s, the SuperMemo World company was first to apply computers to optimize intervals between repetitions. Today, we are still the world leader in research on human long-term memory. Although spaced repetition is common in educational apps nowadays, SuperMemo continues to be the unique source of original research and development in this field.</p>
<p _ngcontent-my-app-c7="">With the SuperMemo method, you can memorize thousands of words, facts and rules, once and for all. The algorithm makes sure that you minimize the time you spend on learning and repetitions, and achieve your learning goals in the most effective way.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block sm-block-dark">
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="container sm-block-container">
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-text">
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-title">
<h2 _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="color-orange">Enjoy high-quality language courses</h2>
</div>
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-desc" prerender="false">
<p _ngcontent-my-app-c7=""><span _ngcontent-my-app-c7="">Our</span> <a _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" href="/en/catalog">collection of over 200 courses</a> <span _ngcontent-my-app-c7="">comprises excellent publications to study more than 10 different languages, and caters for users of various language levels, objectives and styles of learning. Notably, it features the award-winning Olive Green course of English that includes an interactive action film with dialogues designed to teach English from basic to advanced level.</span></p>
<!-- -->
</div>
</div>

<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-title">
<h2 _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="color-orange">Create and share</h2>
</div>
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-desc">
<p _ngcontent-my-app-c7="">You can now develop and publish your own learning content for free, be it for yourself or for all SuperMemo users. Create and share your courses, and learn from flashcards designed by others.</p>
</div>

<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-title">
<h2 _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="color-orange">SuperMemo.com apps</h2>
</div>
<div _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" class="sm-block-desc">
<p _ngcontent-my-app-c7=""><span _ngcontent-my-app-c7="">With</span> <a _ngcontent-my-app-c7="" href="/en/apps">SuperMemo.com apps,</a> <span _ngcontent-my-app-c7="">you can learn both online and offline, and transfer your learning between devices at no extra charge. Download courses from SuperMemo.com online, synchronize and learn anytime on the go.</span></p>
